  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "A schematic real slice of the tube. The coordinate $b = v − ℓ(z)$ is the positive vertical gap; $u$ and the other base directions are suppressed. No convexity of the graph is assumed.",
      "figure_id": "tube:geometry",
      "pdf_page": 7,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "tube:geometry",
          "exact_quote": "A schematic real slice of the tube.  The coordinate\n $b=v-\\ell(z)$ is the positive vertical gap; $u$ and the other base\n directions are suppressed.  No convexity of the graph is assumed.",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1.15cm,y=.85cm]\n \\fill[black!5]\n  (-3,1.8) .. controls (-2.2,.45) and (-1.05,.55) .. (0,1.05)\n  .. controls (1.05,1.55) and (2.2,.65) .. (3,1.45)\n  -- (3,4.15) -- (-3,4.15) -- cycle;\n \\draw[->] (-3.45,0) -- (3.5,0) node[right] {$z$};\n \\draw[->] (-3.35,-.1) -- (-3.35,4.4) node[above] {$v$};\n \\draw[thick]\n  (-3,1.8) .. controls (-2.2,.45) and (-1.05,.55) .. (0,1.05)\n  .. controls (1.05,1.55) and (2.2,.65) .. (3,1.45);\n \\node[anchor=north] at (1.8,.82) {$v=\\ell(z)$};\n \\draw[dashed] (0,0) -- (0,1.05);\n \\draw[<->] (0,1.1) -- (0,3.05)\n  node[midway,right] {$b=v-\\ell(z)$};\n \\fill (0,3.12) circle (1.6pt);\n \\node[above right] at (0,3.12) {$(z,w)$};\n \\node at (-1.75,3.55) {$\\mathcal T_\\ell:\\ b>0$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A schematic coordinate diagram with a vertical axis labeled $v$ and a horizontal axis labeled $z$. A curved profile labeled $v = \\ell(z)$ forms a trough. The region above the curve is labeled $\\mathcal{T}_\\ell : b > 0$. A point $(z, w)$ is shown above the curve, with a vertical line segment of length $b = v - \\ell(z)$ connecting $(z, w)$ to the curve $v = \\ell(z)$, and a dashed extension dropping to the horizontal axis."
    },
    {
      "caption": "One stage, in increasing spatial radius. The test is installed while the spatial growth is capped. The intervening growth and center change occupy finite clock intervals. After the reset, severity must catch up with the accumulated spatial scale before the next capped regime can begin. The arrows indicate order, not elapsed radius.",
      "figure_id": "fig:stage-clock",
      "pdf_page": 44,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:stage-clock",
          "exact_quote": "One stage, in increasing spatial radius. The test is installed\nwhile the spatial growth is capped. The intervening growth and center\nchange occupy finite clock intervals. After the reset, severity must\ncatch up with the accumulated spatial ",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n  stage/.style={draw=black!55,rounded corners=2pt,fill=black!3,\n    text width=3.65cm,minimum height=1.27cm,align=center,font=\\small},\n  flow/.style={-{Latex[length=2mm]},thick,draw=black!65},\n  note/.style={font=\\footnotesize,align=center}]\n\\node[stage] (cap) at (0,0) {Cap and coast\\\\\n  $\\lambda=1$, $H\\le\\sigma$};\n\\node[stage] (trigger) at (4.6,0) {Severity trigger\\\\\n  raise $E$ to $E_*$};\n\\node[stage] (test) at (9.2,0) {Polynomial test\\\\\n  fixed center and severity};\n\\node[stage] (post) at (9.2,-2.05) {Post-test ramp\\\\\n  reach $E_{\\rm post}$};\n\\node[stage] (accelerate) at (4.6,-2.05) {Accelerate growth\\\\\n  enter aligned regime};\n\\node[stage] (grow) at (0,-2.05) {Grow $H$, then $\\lambda$\\\\\n  $E=E_{\\rm post}$};\n\\node[stage] (center) at (0,-4.1) {Widen, move, restore\\\\\n  hold $\\kappa=p=1$};\n\\node[stage] (reset) at (4.6,-4.1) {Return $\\lambda$ to $1$\\\\\n  then lower $H$ to $\\sigma$};\n\\node[stage] (catchup) at (9.2,-4.1) {Severity catch-up\\\\\n  before the next cap};\n\\draw[flow] (cap)--(trigger);\n\\draw[flow] (trigger)--(test);\n\\draw[flow] (test)--(post);\n\\draw[flow] (post)--(accelerate);\n\\draw[flow] (accelerate)--(grow);\n\\draw[flow] (grow)--(center);\n\\draw[flow] (center)--(reset);\n\\draw[flow] (reset)--(catchup);\n\\draw[flow] (catchup.south)--++(0,-.48)\n  node[below,note]{Next stage: cap and coast};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A serpentine flowchart diagram of nine rectangular boxes arranged in a 3-by-3 layout, connected by directional arrows, followed by a final text target:\n- Top row (left-to-right):\n  - Box 1: \"Cap and coast\" / \"$\\lambda = 1, H \\le \\sigma$\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 2: \"Severity trigger\" / \"raise $E$ to $E_*$\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 3: \"Polynomial test\" / \"fixed center\" / \"and severity\"\n- Downward arrow from Box 3 to Box 4.\n- Middle row (right-to-left):\n  - Box 4: \"Post-test ramp\" / \"reach $E_{\\mathrm{post}}$\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 5: \"Accelerate growth\" / \"enter aligned regime\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 6: \"Grow $H$, then $\\lambda$\" / \"$E = E_{\\mathrm{post}}$\"\n- Downward arrow from Box 6 to Box 7.\n- Bottom row (left-to-right):\n  - Box 7: \"Widen, move, restore\" / \"hold $\\kappa = p = 1$\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 8: \"Return $\\lambda$ to 1\" / \"then lower $H$ to $\\sigma$\" $\\rightarrow$\n  - Box 9: \"Severity catch-up\" / \"before the next cap\"\n- Downward arrow from Box 9 to unboxed label: \"Next stage: cap and coast\"."
    }
  ],
